     1→import { useMemo } from 'react';

     4→import { useInsights } from '@/hooks/useInsights';
     5→import { useFilterParams } from '@/hooks/useFilterParams';
     6→import { useProjects } from '@/hooks/useProjects';
     7→import { buildPatternGroups } from '@/lib/pattern-grouping';
     8→import { InsightListItem } from '@/components/insights/InsightListItem';
     9→import { PromptQualityCard } from '@/components/insights/PromptQualityCard';
    10→import { RecurringPatternsSection } from '@/components/insights/RecurringPatternsSection';

    25→import { getDateGroup, sortDateGroups } from '@/lib/utils';
    26→import { INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS } from '@/lib/constants/colors';
    27→import type { Insight, InsightType } from '@/lib/types';
    28→
    29→const INSIGHT_TYPES: InsightType[] = ['summary', 'decision', 'learning', 'technique', 'prompt_quality'];
    30→
    31→const VIEW_MODES = [
    32→  { value: 'timeline', label: 'Timeline' },
    33→  { value: 'type', label: 'By Type' },
    34→  { value: 'project', label: 'By Project' },
    35→  { value: 'session', label: 'By Session' },
    36→] as const;
    37→
    38→interface InsightGroup {
    39→  key: string;
    40→  label: string;
    41→  count: number;
    42→  insights: Insight[];
    43→}
    44→
    45→export default function InsightsPage() {
    46→  const [filters, setFilter, , clearFilters] = useFilterParams({
    47→    q: '',
    48→    project: 'all',
    49→    type: 'all',
    50→    view: 'timeline',
    51→    pattern: '',
    52→  });
    53→
    54→  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    55→  const highlightedInsightId = searchParams.get('insight') || null;
    56→
    57→  const { data: projects = [] } = useProjects();
    58→  const { data: insights = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useInsights(
    59→    filters.project !== 'all' ? { projectId: filters.project } : undefined
    60→  );
    61→
    62→  const allInsightIds = useMemo(() => new Set(insights.map((i) => i.id)), [insights]);
    63→
    64→  const patternGroups = useMemo(() => buildPatternGroups(insights), [insights]);
    65→
    66→  const patternInsightIds = useMemo(() => {
    67→    if (!filters.pattern) return null;
    68→    return patternGroups.get(filters.pattern) ?? null;
    69→  }, [filters.pattern, patternGroups]);
    70→
    71→  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    72→    return insights.filter((i) => {
    73→      if (patternInsightIds && !patternInsightIds.has(i.id)) return false;
    74→      if (filters.type !== 'all' && i.type !== filters.type) return false;
    75→      if (filters.q) {
    76→        const q = filters.q.toLowerCase();
    77→        if (!i.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !i.content.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
    78→          return false;
    79→        }
    80→      }
    81→      return true;
    82→    });
    83→  }, [insights, filters.type, filters.q, patternInsightIds]);
    84→
    85→  const hasFilters = !!filters.q || filters.type !== 'all' || filters.project !== 'all' || !!filters.pattern;
    86→
    87→  const grouped = useMemo((): InsightGroup[] => {
    88→    const view = filters.view;
    89→    const groups = new Map<string, Insight[]>();
    90→
    91→    for (const insight of filtered) {
    92→      let key: string;
    93→      if (view === 'type') {
    94→        key = insight.type;
    95→      } else if (view === 'project') {
    96→        key = insight.project_name;
    97→      } else if (view === 'session') {
    98→        key = insight.session_id;
    99→      } else {
   100→        key = getDateGroup(insight.created_at);
   101→      }
   102→      const arr = groups.get(key) || [];
   103→      arr.push(insight);
   104→      groups.set(key, arr);
   105→    }
   106→
   107→    const entries = [...groups.entries()];
   108→
   109→    if (view === 'timeline') {
   110→      const sorted = sortDateGroups(entries);
   111→      return sorted.map(([key, items]) => ({
   112→        key,
   113→        label: key,
   114→        count: items.length,
   115→        insights: items,
   116→      }));
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    if (view === 'type') {
   120→      return entries.map(([key, items]) => ({
   121→        key,
   122→        label: INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[key as InsightType] || key,
   123→        count: items.length,
   124→        insights: items,
   125→      }));
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    if (view === 'project') {
   129→      entries.sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length);
   130→      return entries.map(([key, items]) => ({
   131→        key,
   132→        label: key,
   133→        count: items.length,
   134→        insights: items,
   135→      }));
   136→    }
   137→
   138→    // session view
   139→    entries.sort((a, b) => {
   140→      const aTime = Math.max(...a[1].map((i) => new Date(i.created_at).getTime()));
   141→      const bTime = Math.max(...b[1].map((i) => new Date(i.created_at).getTime()));
   142→      return bTime - aTime;
   143→    });
   144→    return entries.map(([key, items]) => {
   145→      const first = items[0];
   146→      const sessionDate = format(new Date(first.created_at), 'MMM d, h:mm a');
   147→      return {
   148→        key,
   149→        label: `${first.project_name} -- ${sessionDate}`,
   150→        count: items.length,
   151→        insights: items,
   152→      };
   153→    });
   154→  }, [filtered, filters.view]);
   155→
   156→  return (
   157→    <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
   158→      {/* Header */}
   159→      <div>
   160→        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Insights</h1>
   161→        {!isLoading && (
   162→          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
   163→            {filtered.length} insight{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
   164→            {hasFilters ? ' matching filters' : ''}
   165→          </p>
   166→        )}
   167→      </div>
   168→
   169→      {/* Pattern filter banner */}
   170→      {filters.pattern && (
   171→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-amber-500/5 border-amber-500/20 px-3 py-2">
   172→          <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20">
   173→            Pattern
   174→          </Badge>
   175→          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   176→            Showing {filtered.length} insight{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in this recurring pattern
   177→          </span>
   178→          <Button
   179→            variant="ghost"
   180→            size="icon"
   181→            className="h-6 w-6 ml-auto shrink-0"
   182→            onClick={() => setFilter('pattern', '')}
   183→          >
   184→            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
   185→          </Button>
   186→        </div>
   187→      )}
   188→
   189→      {/* Filters + View Mode */}
   190→      <div className="space-y-2">
   191→        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
   192→          <Input
   193→            placeholder="Search insights..."
   194→            value={filters.q}
   195→            onChange={(e) => setFilter('q', e.target.value)}
   196→            className="max-w-sm"
   197→          />
   198→          <Tabs
   199→            value={filters.view}
   200→            onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('view', v)}
   201→          >
   202→            <TabsList variant="default" className="h-9">
   203→              {VIEW_MODES.map((mode) => (
   204→                <TabsTrigger key={mode.value} value={mode.value} className="text-xs px-3">
   205→                  {mode.label}
   206→                </TabsTrigger>
   207→              ))}
   208→            </TabsList>
   209→          </Tabs>
   210→        </div>
   211→        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
   212→          <Select value={filters.project} onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('project', v)}>
   213→            <SelectTrigger className="w-[160px]">
   214→              <SelectValue placeholder="All Projects" />
   215→            </SelectTrigger>
   216→            <SelectContent>
   217→              <SelectItem value="all">All Projects</SelectItem>
   218→              {projects.map((p) => (
   219→                <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   220→                  {p.name}
   221→                </SelectItem>
   222→              ))}
   223→            </SelectContent>
   224→          </Select>
   225→
   226→          <Select
   227→            value={filters.type}
   228→            onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('type', v)}
   229→          >
   230→            <SelectTrigger className="w-[160px]">
   231→              <SelectValue placeholder="All Types" />
   232→            </SelectTrigger>
   233→            <SelectContent>
   234→              <SelectItem value="all">All Types</SelectItem>
   235→              {INSIGHT_TYPES.map((t) => (
   236→                <SelectItem key={t} value={t}>
   237→                  {INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[t]}
   238→                </SelectItem>
   239→              ))}
   240→            </SelectContent>
   241→          </Select>
   242→        </div>
   243→      </div>
   244→
   245→      {/* Content */}
   246→      {isError && !isLoading ? (
   247→        <ErrorCard message="Failed to load insights" onRetry={refetch} />
   248→      ) : isLoading ? (
   249→        <div className="space-y-3">
   250→          {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => (
   251→            <InsightCardSkeleton key={i} />
   252→          ))}
   253→        </div>
   254→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   255→        hasFilters ? (
   256→          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center space-y-3">
   257→            <SearchX className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
   258→            <p className="font-medium">No insights match your search</p>
   259→            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   260→              Try different keywords or clear the search to see all insights.
   261→            </p>
   262→            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={clearFilters}>

   272→            </p>

   281→          {grouped.map((group) => (

+/** Minimal analyze button for the Prompt Quality empty state. */
+function PromptQualityAnalyzeButton({ session }: { session: Session }) {
+  const { state: analysisState, startAnalysis } = useAnalysis();
+  const { data: llmConfig } = useLlmConfig();
+  const configured = !!(llmConfig?.provider && llmConfig?.model);
+
+  const isAnalyzing =
+    analysisState.status === 'analyzing' && analysisState.sessionId === session.id;
+
+  if (!configured) {
+    return (
+      <Link to="/settings" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground underline hover:text-foreground">
+        Configure AI in Settings
+      </Link>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Button
+      onClick={() => startAnalysis(session, 'prompt_quality')}
+      disabled={isAnalyzing}
+      className="gap-2"
+    >
+      {isAnalyzing ? (
+        <>
+          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
+          Analyzing...
+        </>
+      ) : (
+        <>
+          <Target className="h-4 w-4" />
+          Analyze

+    </Button>

+/**
+ * Format a start–end time range for a sublabel.
+ * Same AM/PM: "7:17 – 8:12 AM"
+ * Different AM/PM: "7:17 AM – 8:12 PM"
+ */
+function formatTimeRange(start: Date, end: Date): string {
+  const startPeriod = format(start, 'a');
+  const endPeriod = format(end, 'a');
+  if (startPeriod === endPeriod) {
+    return `${format(start, 'h:mm')} – ${format(end, 'h:mm a')}`;
+  }
+  return `${format(start, 'h:mm a')} – ${format(end, 'h:mm a')}`;
+}
+
 export function VitalsStrip({ session }: VitalsStripProps) {
   const startedAt = new Date(session.started_at);

+  // Token calculations — fields are independent additive values per Anthropic API convention:
+  // input_tokens = non-cached input; cache tokens are separate counts, not subsets of input
+  const inputTokens = session.total_input_tokens ?? 0;
+  const cacheCreation = session.cache_creation_tokens ?? 0;
+  const cacheRead = session.cache_read_tokens ?? 0;
+  const outputTokens = session.total_output_tokens ?? 0;
+  const totalTokens = inputTokens + cacheCreation + cacheRead + outputTokens;
+  const hasTokens = totalTokens > 0;
+
+  // Build token sublabel: "359 in · 25.9M cch · 51.2K out"
+  const tokenParts: string[] = [];
+  if (inputTokens > 0) tokenParts.push(`${formatTokenCount(inputTokens)} in`);
+  const cacheTotal = cacheCreation + cacheRead;
+  if (cacheTotal > 0) tokenParts.push(`${formatTokenCount(cacheTotal)} cch`);
+  if (outputTokens > 0) tokenParts.push(`${formatTokenCount(outputTokens)} out`);
+  const tokenSublabel = tokenParts.join(' \u00B7 ');
+
   return (

   160→
   161→              {bullets.length > 0 && (
   162→                <ul className="mt-3 space-y-1">
   163→                  {bullets.map((bullet, i) => (

   165→                      <span className="shrink-0 mt-0.5">-</span>
   166→                      <span>{bullet}</span>
   167→                    </li>
   168→                  ))}
   169→                </ul>
   170→              )}
   171→            </>
   172→          )}
   173→
   174→          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   175→            <span>Confidence: {Math.round(insight.confidence * 100)}%</span>
   176→          </div>
   177→
   178→          <div className="mt-3">
   179→            <a
   180→              href={`/sessions?session=${insight.session_id}`}
   181→              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground underline-offset-2 hover:underline transition-colors"
   182→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   183→            >
   184→              <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
   185→              View session
   186→            </a>
   187→          </div>
   188→        </div>
   189→      )}
   190→    </div>
   191→  );
   192→}

    10→// Re-export from shared module for backward compat (SessionDetailPage, SessionsPage import these)
    11→export { OutcomeBadge } from './insight-metadata';
    12→
    13→interface InsightCardProps {
    14→  insight: Insight;
    15→  showProject?: boolean;
    16→  allInsightIds?: Set<string>;
    17→}
    18→
    19→const typeIcons: Record<InsightType, typeof FileText> = {
    20→  summary: FileText,

    23→  technique: BookOpen,
    24→  prompt_quality: Target,
    25→};
    26→
    27→export function InsightCard({ insight, showProject = false, allInsightIds }: InsightCardProps) {
    28→  const Icon = typeIcons[insight.type];
    29→  const bullets = parseJsonField<string[]>(insight.bullets, []);
    30→  const metadata = parseJsonField<InsightMetadata>(insight.metadata, {});
    31→  const linkedIds = insight.linked_insight_ids
    32→    ? parseJsonField<string[]>(insight.linked_insight_ids, [])
    33→    : [];
    34→
    35→  const recurringCount = linkedIds.length > 0
    36→    ? (allInsightIds
    37→        ? linkedIds.filter(id => allInsightIds.has(id)).length
    38→        : linkedIds.length)
    39→    : 0;
    40→
    41→  const evidence = Array.isArray(metadata.evidence) ? metadata.evidence : [];
    42→
    43→  // Try type-specific rendering; fall back to generic bullets
    44→  const typeContent = renderTypeContent(insight.type, metadata, bullets);
    45→
    46→  return (
    47→    <Card>
    48→      <CardHeader className="pb-2">
    49→        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
    50→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
    51→            <div className={`rounded-md p-1.5 ${INSIGHT_TYPE_COLORS[insight.type]}`}>
    52→              <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
    53→            </div>
    54→            <div>
    55→              <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium line-clamp-2">
    56→                {insight.title}
    57→              </CardTitle>
    58→              {showProject && (
    59→                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{insight.project_name}</p>
    60→              )}
    61→            </div>
    62→          </div>
    63→          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
    64→            <Badge variant="outline" className={INSIGHT_TYPE_COLORS[insight.type]}>
    65→              {INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[insight.type]}
    66→            </Badge>
    67→            {recurringCount > 0 && (
    68→              <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20">
    69→                Recurring ({recurringCount + 1}x)
    70→              </Badge>
    71→            )}
    72→          </div>
    73→        </div>
    74→      </CardHeader>
    75→      <CardContent>
    76→        {typeContent || (
    77→          bullets.length > 0 ? (
    78→            <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
    79→              {bullets.slice(0, 3).map((bullet, i) => (
    80→                <li key={i} className="line-clamp-1">{bullet}</li>
    81→              ))}
    82→              {bullets.length > 3 && (
    83→                <li className="text-muted-foreground/70">
    84→                  +{bullets.length - 3} more...
    85→                </li>
    86→              )}
    87→            </ul>
    88→          ) : (
    89→            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground line-clamp-3">
    90→              {insight.summary || insight.content}
    91→            </p>
    92→          )
    93→        )}
    94→        {evidence.length > 0 && (
    95→          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2">
    96→            Evidence: {evidence.join(', ')}
    97→          </p>
    98→        )}
    99→        <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between">
   100→          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   101→            {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(insight.timestamp), { addSuffix: true })}
   102→          </span>
   103→        </div>
   104→      </CardContent>
   105→    </Card>
   106→  );
   107→}

     8→interface PromptQualityCardProps {

    50→

    53→const SCORE_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {

    59→
    60→function getScoreColor(score: number): string {

    63→
    64→export function PromptQualityCard({ insight }: PromptQualityCardProps) {
    65→  const metadata = parseJsonField<Record<string, unknown>>(insight.metadata, {});

    71→  const sessionTraits = Array.isArray(metadata.sessionTraits) ? metadata.sessionTraits as SessionTrait[] : [];
    72→  const reduction = typeof metadata.potentialMessageReduction === 'number' ? metadata.potentialMessageReduction : 0;
    73→
    74→  return (
    75→    <Card className="border-rose-500/20">
    76→      <CardHeader className="pb-3">
    77→        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">

    81→            </div>

   102→            </p>
   103→          </div>

   105→
   106→        {reduction > 0 && (

   109→            <span>

   170→                  {t.suggestion && (

   175→            </div>

   178→
   179→        {wastedTurns.length > 0 && (

     3→import { useProjects } from '@/hooks/useProjects';

    23→  return () => lgQuery?.removeEventListener('change', cb);
    24→}
    25→function getIsLg() {

    27→}
    28→
    29→export default function SessionsPage() {
    30→  const [filters, setFilter, setFilters, clearFilters] = useFilterParams({
    31→    q: '',
    32→    project: 'all',
    33→    source: 'all',
    34→    character: 'all',
    35→    status: 'all',
    36→    session: '',
    37→  });
    38→
    39→  const { data: projects = [], isLoading: projectsLoading } = useProjects();
    40→
    41→  const sessionParams = useMemo(() => {
    42→    const params: { projectId?: string; sourceTool?: string; limit?: number } = { limit: 200 };
    43→    if (filters.project !== 'all') params.projectId = filters.project;
    44→    if (filters.source !== 'all') params.sourceTool = filters.source;
    45→    return params;
    46→  }, [filters.project, filters.source]);
    47→
    48→  const { data: sessions = [], isLoading: sessionsLoading } = useSessions(sessionParams);
    49→  const { data: insights = [], isLoading: insightsLoading } = useInsights();
    50→
    51→  const loading = sessionsLoading || projectsLoading || insightsLoading;
    52→
    53→  const handleSelectProject = useCallback(
    54→    (projectId: string) => {
    55→      const currentSessionId = filters.session;
    56→      if (currentSessionId && projectId !== 'all') {
    57→        const currentSession = sessions.find((s) => s.id === currentSessionId);
    58→        if (currentSession && currentSession.project_id !== projectId) {
    59→          setFilters({ project: projectId, session: '' });
    60→          return;
    61→        }
    62→      }
    63→      setFilter('project', projectId);
    64→    },
    65→    [filters.session, sessions, setFilter, setFilters]
    66→  );
    67→
    68→  const handleSelectSession = useCallback(
    69→    (sessionId: string) => {
    70→      setFilter('session', sessionId);
    71→    },
    72→    [setFilter]
    73→  );
    74→
    75→  const handleFilterChange = useCallback(
    76→    (key: 'q' | 'character' | 'status', value: string) => {
    77→      setFilter(key, value);
    78→    },
    79→    [setFilter]
    80→  );

    83→    setFilters({ q: '', character: 'all', status: 'all' });

    85→

    87→    if (filters.project === 'all') return 'All Projects';
    88→    return projects.find((p) => p.id === filters.project)?.name ?? 'Project';

    90→
    91→  const showProject = filters.project === 'all';
    92→  const isLg = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeLg, getIsLg);
    93→
    94→  return (
    95→    <div className="flex h-[calc(100vh-3.5rem)]">
    96→      {/* Panel A: Project Nav — visible at xl, Sheet at lg, hidden below */}
    97→      <aside className="hidden xl:flex w-[220px] shrink-0 border-r bg-background overflow-y-auto">
    98→        <div className="w-full">
    99→          <ProjectNav
   100→            projects={projects}
   101→            selectedProject={filters.project}
   102→            selectedSource={filters.source}
   103→            onSelectProject={handleSelectProject}
   104→            onSelectSource={(source) => setFilter('source', source)}
   105→          />
   106→        </div>
   107→      </aside>
   108→
   109→      {/* Panel B: Session List */}
   110→      <div className="w-full lg:w-80 shrink-0 lg:border-r bg-background flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
   111→        {/* Project selector for lg (Sheet trigger) and below xl */}
   112→        <div className="xl:hidden shrink-0 px-3 pt-3 pb-1">
   113→          <Sheet>
   114→            <SheetTrigger asChild>
   115→              <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="w-full justify-between text-xs h-8">
   116→                <span className="truncate">{selectedProjectName}</span>
   117→                <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 ml-1" />
   118→              </Button>
   119→            </SheetTrigger>

   188→            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
   189→              <SessionDetailPanel sessionId={filters.session} />

   191→          </SheetContent>

   195→  );
   196→}

     1→import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { Link } from 'react-router';
     3→import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
     4→import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
     5→import { Repeat2 } from 'lucide-react';
     6→import { INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS } from '@/lib/constants/colors';
     7→import { buildPatternGroups } from '@/lib/pattern-grouping';
     8→import type { Insight, InsightType } from '@/lib/types';
     9→
    10→interface RecurringPatternsSectionProps {
    11→  insights: Insight[];
    12→}
    13→
    14→interface PatternGroup {
    15→  key: string;
    16→  title: string;
    17→  type: string;
    18→  count: number;
    19→  projects: string[];
    20→  lastSeen: string;
    21→  insightIds: Set<string>;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export function RecurringPatternsSection({ insights }: RecurringPatternsSectionProps) {
    25→  const patterns = useMemo((): PatternGroup[] => {
    26→    const insightMap = new Map<string, Insight>();
    27→    for (const insight of insights) {
    28→      insightMap.set(insight.id, insight);
    29→    }
    30→
    31→    const groups = buildPatternGroups(insights);
    32→
    33→    // Filter to groups with 2+ insights and build PatternGroup objects
    34→    const result: PatternGroup[] = [];
    35→    for (const [key, ids] of groups) {
    36→      if (ids.size < 2) continue;
    37→
    38→      const groupInsights = [...ids]
    39→        .map((id) => insightMap.get(id))
    40→        .filter((i): i is Insight => !!i);
    41→
    42→      if (groupInsights.length < 2) continue;
    43→
    44→      // Most common type in group
    45→      const typeCounts = new Map<string, number>();
    46→      for (const i of groupInsights) {
    47→        typeCounts.set(i.type, (typeCounts.get(i.type) || 0) + 1);
    48→      }
    49→      const dominantType = [...typeCounts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
    50→
    51→      const projects = [...new Set(groupInsights.map((i) => i.project_name))];
    52→      const lastSeen = groupInsights
    53→        .map((i) => i.created_at)
    54→        .sort()
    55→        .pop()!;
    56→
    57→      result.push({
    58→        key,
    59→        title: groupInsights[0].title,
    60→        type: dominantType,
    61→        count: groupInsights.length,
    62→        projects,
    63→        lastSeen,
    64→        insightIds: ids,
    65→      });
    66→    }
    67→
    68→    return result.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
    69→  }, [insights]);
    70→
    71→  if (patterns.length === 0) return null;
    72→
    73→  return (
    74→    <div>
    75→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
    76→        <Repeat2 className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
    77→        <h2 className="text-sm font-medium">Recurring Patterns</h2>
    78→        <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
    79→          {patterns.length}
    80→        </Badge>
    81→      </div>
    82→      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
    83→        {patterns.map((pattern) => (
    84→          <Link
    85→            key={pattern.key}
    86→            to={`/insights?pattern=${pattern.key}`}
    87→            className="block rounded-lg border p-3 hover:bg-accent/40 transition-colors space-y-1.5"
    88→          >
    89→            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
    90→              <p className="text-sm font-medium line-clamp-2">{pattern.title}</p>
    91→              <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20 shrink-0">
    92→                {pattern.count}x
    93→              </Badge>
    94→            </div>
    95→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
    96→              <span>{INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[pattern.type as InsightType] || pattern.type}</span>
    97→              <span>--</span>
    98→              <span className="truncate">{pattern.projects.join(', ')}</span>
    99→            </div>
   100→            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   101→              Last seen {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(pattern.lastSeen), { addSuffix: true })}
   102→            </p>
   103→          </Link>
   104→        ))}
   105→      </div>
   106→    </div>
   107→  );
   108→}
   109→
```

     1→/**
     2→ * Shared prompt quality score tier logic.
     3→ * Single source of truth for threshold values used across:
     4→ * - CompactSessionRow (list badge)
     5→ * - SessionDetailPanel (tab badge)
     6→ * - PromptQualityCard (score display)
     7→ * - ProgressRing (SVG ring)
     8→ */
     9→
    10→export type ScoreTier = 'excellent' | 'good' | 'fair' | 'poor';
    11→
    12→export function getScoreTier(score: number): ScoreTier {
    13→  if (score >= 80) return 'excellent';
    14→  if (score >= 60) return 'good';
    15→  if (score >= 40) return 'fair';
    16→  return 'poor';
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function getScoreLabel(score: number): string {
    20→  if (score >= 80) return 'Excellent';
    21→  if (score >= 60) return 'Good';
    22→  if (score >= 40) return 'Needs Improvement';
    23→  return 'Poor';
    24→}
    25→

---

## Sessions UX Polish — What Was Done (PR #100)

The Sessions page received these key improvements:

1. **VitalsStrip redesign** — Replaced token/model row with a clean 4-column grid: Duration (with time range sublabel), Messages, Tokens (with in/cch/out breakdown), Cost (with model name sublabel)
2. **Tab restructure** — Changed from "Overview | Conversation" to "Insights (N) | Prompt Quality [score badge] | Conversation (N)" using `variant="line"` tabs
3. **CollapsibleInsightItem restyle** — Compact row with colored dot indicator, border-left accent on expand, no more hint labels
4. **Prompt Quality as dedicated tab** — Separated from the insights list, with empty state + analyze button
5. **Learnings/Decisions grouped** — Colored section icons (green for learnings, blue for decisions), wrapped in a single `rounded-md border` container
6. **CompactSessionRow** — Added prompt quality score display with colored Target icon
7. **Tool count in header** — Added wrench icon + tool count to session metadata strip
8. **Export icon** — Changed FileText to Download icon

## Insights Page — Current State vs Proposed Polish

Comparing the screenshots, the Insights page is functional but visually less polished. Here's what I'd propose, modeled on the Sessions page improvements:

### Changes

| # | Change | Files |
|---|--------|-------|
| 1 | **Compact insight rows** — Make `InsightListItem` more compact: remove the large icon box, use a small colored dot (like CollapsibleInsightItem in Sessions). Tighten padding. | `InsightListItem.tsx` |
| 2 | **Expand restyle** — Use the same border-left accent pattern from Sessions for expanded insight content (colored left border, muted bg). | `InsightListItem.tsx` |
| 3 | **Section headers polish** — Use colored icons for type section headers (green for learnings, blue for decisions, etc.) matching Sessions pattern. Date group headers already look fine. | `InsightsPage.tsx` |
| 4 | **Filter bar consolidation** — Move filters into a single row: Search + View Mode tabs + Project dropdown + Type dropdown (currently split across 2 rows). | `InsightsPage.tsx` |
| 5 | **PromptQualityCard in list** — Add score badge inline (similar to tab badge in Sessions) on prompt_quality items in the list view. | `PromptQualityCard.tsx` or `InsightsPage.tsx` |
| 6 | **Grouped container** — When in "By Type" or "By Session" view, wrap items in a single bordered container (like Learnings/Decisions in Sessions) instead of individual bordered cards with gaps. | `InsightsPage.tsx` |

### Files to modify
- `dashboard/src/components/insights/InsightListItem.tsx` — compact row + expand restyle
- `dashboard/src/pages/InsightsPage.tsx` — filter consolidation, grouped containers, section icon colors

### Not changing
- `PromptQualityCard.tsx` — already well-designed, no changes needed
- `RecurringPatternsSection.tsx` — already clean
- No new files needed

Want me to proceed with this plan?

w-4\" />}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </button>\n\n      {expanded && (\n        <div className={cn(\n          'mx-3 mb-2.5 ml-[1.85rem] pl-3 pr-3 py-2.5 border-l-2 bg-muted/20 rounded-r-md',\n          EXPAND_BORDER_COLORS[insight.type]\n        )}>\n          {expandedTypeContent ? (\n            <div>{expandedTypeContent}</div>\n          ) : (\n            <>\n              {insight.content && (\n                <p className=\"text-sm text-foreground leading-relaxed\">{insight.content}</p>\n              )}\n              {bullets.length > 0 && (\n                <ul className=\"mt-2 space-y-1\">\n                  {bullets.map((bullet, i) => (\n                    <li key={i} className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground flex gap-2\">\n                      <span className=\"shrink-0 mt-0.5\">-</span>\n                      <span>{bullet}</span>\n                    </li>\n                  ))}\n                </ul>\n              )}\n            </>\n          )}\n\n          {metadata.outcome && insight.type === 'summary' && (\n            <div className=\"mt-2\">\n              <OutcomeBadge outcome={metadata.outcome} />\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <div className=\"mt-3\">\n            <a\n              href={`/sessions?session=${insight.session_id}`}\n              className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground underline-offset-2 hover:underline transition-colors\"\n              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}\n            >\n              <ExternalLink className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n              View session\n            </a>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

     1→import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { format } from 'date-fns';
     3→import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
     4→import { useInsights } from '@/hooks/useInsights';
     5→import { useFilterParams } from '@/hooks/useFilterParams';
     6→import { useProjects } from '@/hooks/useProjects';
     7→import { buildPatternGroups } from '@/lib/pattern-grouping';
     8→import { InsightListItem } from '@/components/insights/InsightListItem';
     9→import { PromptQualityCard } from '@/components/insights/PromptQualityCard';
    10→import { RecurringPatternsSection } from '@/components/insights/RecurringPatternsSection';
    11→import { InsightCardSkeleton } from '@/components/skeletons/InsightCardSkeleton';
    12→import { ErrorCard } from '@/components/ErrorCard';
    13→import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
    14→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
    15→import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
    16→import { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs';
    17→import {
    18→  Select,
    19→  SelectContent,
    20→  SelectItem,
    21→  SelectTrigger,
    22→  SelectValue,
    23→} from '@/components/ui/select';
    24→import { Sparkles, SearchX, X, FileText, GitCommit, BookOpen, Target } from 'lucide-react';
    25→import { getDateGroup, sortDateGroups } from '@/lib/utils';
    26→import { INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS } from '@/lib/constants/colors';
    27→import type { Insight, InsightType } from '@/lib/types';
    28→
    29→const INSIGHT_TYPES: InsightType[] = ['summary', 'decision', 'learning', 'technique', 'prompt_quality'];
    30→
    31→const TYPE_SECTION_ICONS: Record<string, { icon: typeof FileText; color: string }> = {
    32→  summary: { icon: FileText, color: 'text-purple-500' },
    33→  decision: { icon: GitCommit, color: 'text-blue-500' },
    34→  learning: { icon: BookOpen, color: 'text-green-500' },
    35→  technique: { icon: BookOpen, color: 'text-green-500' },
    36→  prompt_quality: { icon: Target, color: 'text-rose-500' },
    37→};
    38→
    39→const VIEW_MODES = [
    40→  { value: 'timeline', label: 'Timeline' },
    41→  { value: 'type', label: 'By Type' },
    42→  { value: 'project', label: 'By Project' },
    43→  { value: 'session', label: 'By Session' },
    44→] as const;
    45→
    46→interface InsightGroup {
    47→  key: string;
    48→  label: string;
    49→  count: number;
    50→  insights: Insight[];
    51→}
    52→
    53→export default function InsightsPage() {
    54→  const [filters, setFilter, , clearFilters] = useFilterParams({
    55→    q: '',
    56→    project: 'all',
    57→    type: 'all',
    58→    view: 'timeline',
    59→    pattern: '',
    60→  });
    61→
    62→  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    63→  const highlightedInsightId = searchParams.get('insight') || null;
    64→
    65→  const { data: projects = [] } = useProjects();
    66→  const { data: insights = [], isLoading, isError, refetch } = useInsights(
    67→    filters.project !== 'all' ? { projectId: filters.project } : undefined
    68→  );
    69→
    70→  const allInsightIds = useMemo(() => new Set(insights.map((i) => i.id)), [insights]);
    71→
    72→  const patternGroups = useMemo(() => buildPatternGroups(insights), [insights]);
    73→
    74→  const patternInsightIds = useMemo(() => {
    75→    if (!filters.pattern) return null;
    76→    return patternGroups.get(filters.pattern) ?? null;
    77→  }, [filters.pattern, patternGroups]);
    78→
    79→  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    80→    return insights.filter((i) => {
    81→      if (patternInsightIds && !patternInsightIds.has(i.id)) return false;
    82→      if (filters.type !== 'all' && i.type !== filters.type) return false;
    83→      if (filters.q) {
    84→        const q = filters.q.toLowerCase();
    85→        if (!i.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !i.content.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
    86→          return false;
    87→        }
    88→      }
    89→      return true;
    90→    });
    91→  }, [insights, filters.type, filters.q, patternInsightIds]);
    92→
    93→  const hasFilters = !!filters.q || filters.type !== 'all' || filters.project !== 'all' || !!filters.pattern;
    94→
    95→  const grouped = useMemo((): InsightGroup[] => {
    96→    const view = filters.view;
    97→    const groups = new Map<string, Insight[]>();
    98→
    99→    for (const insight of filtered) {
   100→      let key: string;
   101→      if (view === 'type') {
   102→        key = insight.type;
   103→      } else if (view === 'project') {
   104→        key = insight.project_name;
   105→      } else if (view === 'session') {
   106→        key = insight.session_id;
   107→      } else {
   108→        key = getDateGroup(insight.created_at);
   109→      }
   110→      const arr = groups.get(key) || [];
   111→      arr.push(insight);
   112→      groups.set(key, arr);
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    const entries = [...groups.entries()];
   116→
   117→    if (view === 'timeline') {
   118→      const sorted = sortDateGroups(entries);
   119→      return sorted.map(([key, items]) => ({
   120→        key,
   121→        label: key,
   122→        count: items.length,
   123→        insights: items,
   124→      }));
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    if (view === 'type') {
   128→      return entries.map(([key, items]) => ({
   129→        key,
   130→        label: INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[key as InsightType] || key,
   131→        count: items.length,
   132→        insights: items,
   133→      }));
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    if (view === 'project') {
   137→      entries.sort((a, b) => b[1].length - a[1].length);
   138→      return entries.map(([key, items]) => ({
   139→        key,
   140→        label: key,
   141→        count: items.length,
   142→        insights: items,
   143→      }));
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    // session view
   147→    entries.sort((a, b) => {
   148→      const aTime = Math.max(...a[1].map((i) => new Date(i.created_at).getTime()));
   149→      const bTime = Math.max(...b[1].map((i) => new Date(i.created_at).getTime()));
   150→      return bTime - aTime;
   151→    });
   152→    return entries.map(([key, items]) => {
   153→      const first = items[0];
   154→      const sessionDate = format(new Date(first.created_at), 'MMM d, h:mm a');
   155→      return {
   156→        key,
   157→        label: `${first.project_name} -- ${sessionDate}`,
   158→        count: items.length,
   159→        insights: items,
   160→      };
   161→    });
   162→  }, [filtered, filters.view]);
   163→
   164→  return (
   165→    <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
   166→      {/* Header */}
   167→      <div>
   168→        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Insights</h1>
   169→        {!isLoading && (
   170→          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
   171→            {filtered.length} insight{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
   172→            {hasFilters ? ' matching filters' : ''}
   173→          </p>
   174→        )}
   175→      </div>
   176→
   177→      {/* Pattern filter banner */}
   178→      {filters.pattern && (
   179→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-amber-500/5 border-amber-500/20 px-3 py-2">
   180→          <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 border-amber-500/20">
   181→            Pattern
   182→          </Badge>
   183→          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   184→            Showing {filtered.length} insight{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in this recurring pattern
   185→          </span>
   186→          <Button
   187→            variant="ghost"
   188→            size="icon"
   189→            className="h-6 w-6 ml-auto shrink-0"
   190→            onClick={() => setFilter('pattern', '')}
   191→          >
   192→            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
   193→          </Button>
   194→        </div>
   195→      )}
   196→
   197→      {/* Filters + View Mode */}
   198→      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
   199→        <Input
   200→          placeholder="Search insights..."
   201→          value={filters.q}
   202→          onChange={(e) => setFilter('q', e.target.value)}
   203→          className="max-w-xs"
   204→        />
   205→
   206→        <Select value={filters.project} onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('project', v)}>
   207→          <SelectTrigger className="w-[150px]">
   208→            <SelectValue placeholder="All Projects" />
   209→          </SelectTrigger>
   210→          <SelectContent>
   211→            <SelectItem value="all">All Projects</SelectItem>
   212→            {projects.map((p) => (
   213→              <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   214→                {p.name}
   215→              </SelectItem>
   216→            ))}
   217→          </SelectContent>
   218→        </Select>
   219→
   220→        <Select value={filters.type} onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('type', v)}>
   221→          <SelectTrigger className="w-[150px]">
   222→            <SelectValue placeholder="All Types" />
   223→          </SelectTrigger>
   224→          <SelectContent>
   225→            <SelectItem value="all">All Types</SelectItem>
   226→            {INSIGHT_TYPES.map((t) => (
   227→              <SelectItem key={t} value={t}>
   228→                {INSIGHT_TYPE_LABELS[t]}
   229→              </SelectItem>
   230→            ))}
   231→          </SelectContent>
   232→        </Select>
   233→
   234→        <Tabs
   235→          value={filters.view}
   236→          onValueChange={(v) => setFilter('view', v)}
   237→          className="ml-auto"
   238→        >
   239→          <TabsList variant="default" className="h-9">
   240→            {VIEW_MODES.map((mode) => (
   241→              <TabsTrigger key={mode.value} value={mode.value} className="text-xs px-3">
   242→                {mode.label}
   243→              </TabsTrigger>
   244→            ))}
   245→          </TabsList>
   246→        </Tabs>
   247→      </div>
   248→
   249→      {/* Content */}
   250→      {isError && !isLoading ? (
   251→        <ErrorCard message="Failed to load insights" onRetry={refetch} />
   252→      ) : isLoading ? (
   253→        <div className="space-y-3">
   254→          {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => (
   255→            <InsightCardSkeleton key={i} />
   256→          ))}
   257→        </div>
   258→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   259→        hasFilters ? (
   260→          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center space-y-3">
   261→            <SearchX className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
   262→            <p className="font-medium">No insights match your search</p>
   263→            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   264→              Try different keywords or clear the search to see all insights.
   265→            </p>
   266→            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={clearFilters}>
   267→              Clear filters
   268→            </Button>
   269→          </div>
   270→        ) : (
   271→          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center space-y-3">
   272→            <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
   273→            <p className="font-medium">No insights yet</p>
   274→            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
   275→              Analyze your sessions to generate AI insights like learnings, decisions, and summaries.
   276→            </p>
   277→          </div>
   278→        )
   279→      ) : (
   280→        <div className="space-y-6">
   281→          {!filters.pattern && (
   282→            <RecurringPatternsSection insights={insights} />
   283→          )}
   284→
   285→          {grouped.map((group) => {
   286→            const sectionMeta = filters.view === 'type' ? TYPE_SECTION_ICONS[group.key] : null;
   287→            const SectionIcon = sectionMeta?.icon;
   288→
   289→            // Separate prompt_quality cards (rendered standalone) from list items
   290→            const promptQualityInsights = group.insights.filter((i) => i.type === 'prompt_quality');
   291→            const listInsights = group.insights.filter((i) => i.type !== 'prompt_quality');
   292→
   293→            return (
   294→              <div key={group.key}>
   295→                <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground mb-2">
   296→                  {SectionIcon && <SectionIcon className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${sectionMeta.color}`} />}
   297→                  {group.label} ({group.count})
   298→                </h2>
   299→
   300→                {promptQualityInsights.map((insight) => (
   301→                  <PromptQualityCard key={insight.id} insight={insight} />
   302→                ))}
   303→
   304→                {listInsights.length > 0 && (
   305→                  <div className="rounded-md border overflow-hidden">
   306→                    {listInsights.map((insight) => (
   307→                      <InsightListItem
   308→                        key={insight.id}
   309→                        insight={insight}
   310→                        showProject={filters.view !== 'project'}
   311→                        allInsightIds={allInsightIds}
   312→                        highlighted={insight.id === highlightedInsightId}
   313→                        defaultExpanded={insight.id === highlightedInsightId}
   314→                      />
   315→                    ))}
   316→                  </div>
   317→                )}
   318→              </div>
   319→            );
   320→          })}
   321→        </div>
   322→      )}
   323→    </div>
   324→  );
   325→}
   326→
```

> AGENT

Only used in `InsightsPage.tsx` — no other consumers. Build already passed. Here's a summary of what changed:

---

## Changes Made

### `InsightListItem.tsx` — Compact row + expand restyle
- **Large icon box → colored dot** — Replaced the padded icon container with a small 2x2 colored dot (purple for summary, blue for decision, green for learning/technique, rose for prompt quality)
- **Tighter padding** — `p-4` → `px-3 py-2.5` for a more compact list feel
- **Outer wrapper** — Changed from `border rounded-lg` to `border-b last:border-b-0` so items sit inside a grouped container
- **Expanded content** — Now uses a border-left accent with type-colored border + muted bg (matching the Sessions panel pattern)
- **Removed** collapsed preview text and confidence percentage (declutters the list)
- **Kept** type label, title, project name, relative time, recurring badge, view session link, highlight/scroll behavior

### `InsightsPage.tsx` — Filter consolidation + grouped containers
- **Single filter row** — Merged the 2-row filter layout into one flex row: Search → Project dropdown → Type dropdown → View Mode tabs (pushed right with `ml-auto`)
- **Grouped containers** — Wrapped each group's insight items in a `rounded-md border overflow-hidden` container, so items appear as rows within a card instead of separate floating cards
- **Colored section icons** — In "By Type" view, section headers now show type-colored icons (purple FileText for Summary, blue GitCommit for Decision, etc.)
- **PromptQuality separation** — Prompt quality insights render as standalone `PromptQualityCard` above the grouped list items (since they have their own rich card layout)

PromptQualityCardProps) {\n  return (\n    <Card className=\"border-rose-500/20\">\n      <CardHeader className=\"pb-3\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <div className=\"rounded-md p-1.5 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500 border-rose-500/20\">\n              <Target className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n            </div>\n            <CardTitle className=\"text-base\">Prompt Quality Analysis</CardTitle>\n          </div>\n          <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-500 border-rose-500/20\">\n            Prompt Quality\n          </Badge>\n        </div>\n      </CardHeader>\n\n      <CardContent>\n        <PromptQualityContent insight={insight} />\n      </CardContent>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}

     1→---
     2→title: "The Dashboard"
     3→description: "The embedded browser dashboard served by the CLI at localhost:7890."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## Launching the dashboard
     7→
     8→```bash
     9→code-insights dashboard
    10→```
    11→
    12→Opens your browser at `localhost:7890`. The dashboard is a Vite + React SPA served by the Hono server embedded in the CLI. It runs as long as the terminal process is alive.
    13→
    14→## Navigation
    15→
    16→| Page | What it shows |
    17→|------|---------------|
    18→| Dashboard | Activity chart, session stats, unanalyzed session count, bulk analyze button |
    19→| Sessions | Searchable session list with filters by project, source tool, and character |
    20→| Insights | AI-generated decisions, learnings, techniques with type and project filters |
    21→| Analytics | Charts: activity over time, model usage, cost breakdown, token trends |
    22→| Journal | Weekly learning timeline with recurring patterns detection |
    23→| Export | Download sessions and insights as Markdown (by project or date range) |
    24→| Settings | LLM provider configuration with credential testing and Ollama model discovery |
    25→
    26→## LLM configuration
    27→
    28→To enable AI-powered insights:
    29→
    30→1. Open **Settings** in the dashboard
    31→2. Select a provider: OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, or Ollama
    32→3. Enter your API key
    33→4. Return to any session and click **Analyze**
    34→
    35→Ollama runs fully locally — no API key required. Install Ollama and start a model:
    36→
    37→```bash
    38→ollama pull llama3.2
    39→```
    40→
    41→Then select **Ollama** in Settings and enter your model name.
    42→
    43→## Port configuration
    44→
    45→If port 7890 is already in use, the dashboard will fail to start. Check what's using the port:
    46→
    47→```bash
    48→lsof -i :7890
    49→```
    50→
```

```
     1→---
     2→title: "Insights & Analysis"
     3→description: "LLM-powered analysis of your coding sessions."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## What insights are
     7→
     8→When you analyze a session, the CLI server sends the conversation to your configured LLM provider and extracts structured insights. These are stored in the local SQLite database and displayed in the dashboard.
     9→
    10→The analysis uses a "senior staff engineer writing for a knowledge base" persona — each insight is written as if a future developer will read it 6 months from now with no context about the session.
    11→
    12→## The 5 insight types
    13→
    14→| Type | What it captures |
    15→|------|-----------------|
    16→| **Summary** | Narrative of what was accomplished, outcome status (success/partial/abandoned/blocked), and key artifacts changed |
    17→| **Decision** | A technical choice with full context: situation, reasoning, rejected alternatives, trade-offs, and conditions for revisiting |
    18→| **Learning** | A technical discovery: observable symptom, root cause, transferable takeaway, and when it applies |
    19→| **Technique** | A reusable approach or pattern demonstrated |
    20→| **Prompt Quality** | Efficiency score, wasted turns, anti-patterns, and session behavioral traits |
    21→
    22→### Decision structure
    23→
    24→Each decision insight includes:
    25→
    26→- **Situation** — what problem or requirement led to the decision
    27→- **Choice** — what was chosen and how it was implemented
    28→- **Reasoning** — the key factors that tipped the decision
    29→- **Alternatives** — rejected options with reasons why
    30→- **Trade-offs** — what downsides were accepted
    31→- **Revisit when** — conditions under which to reconsider
    32→
    33→### Learning structure
    34→
    35→Each learning insight includes:
    36→
    37→- **Symptom** — what went wrong or was confusing
    38→- **Root cause** — the underlying technical reason
    39→- **Takeaway** — the transferable lesson for similar situations
    40→- **Applies when** — conditions under which this knowledge is relevant
    41→
    42→## Running analysis
    43→
    44→1. Open the dashboard: `code-insights dashboard`
    45→2. Navigate to **Sessions**
    46→3. Click into any session
    47→4. Click **Analyze** (requires LLM configured in Settings)
    48→
    49→Analysis runs server-side via the Hono API. Progress is streamed back to the dashboard in real time via Server-Sent Events (SSE), so you can see each insight as it's extracted. Your API key stays on your machine and is never sent anywhere else.
    50→
    51→During analysis, the LLM also classifies the session into one of 7 character types (deep_focus, bug_hunt, feature_build, etc.) and writes it back to the session record.
    52→
    53→## Bulk analysis
    54→
    55→From the **Dashboard** page, click **Analyze** on the unanalyzed sessions card to run analysis across all pending sessions. You can also analyze individual sessions from the session detail page. Progress is shown in real time via SSE streaming.
    56→
    57→## Prompt quality analysis
    58→
    59→The prompt quality analysis evaluates your prompting efficiency on a 0-100 scale.
    60→
    61→### What it detects
    62→
    63→- **Wasted turns** — messages that led to unnecessary clarifications or corrections, with the original message, what went wrong, and a suggested rewrite
    64→- **Anti-patterns** — recurring bad habits (e.g., vague instructions, missing file paths) with specific fixes
    65→- **Session traits** — higher-level behavioral patterns about how the session was structured:
    66→
    67→| Trait | What it means |
    68→|-------|--------------|
    69→| `context_drift` | Session covers too many unrelated objectives, causing the AI to lose context |
    70→| `objective_bloat` | Too many different tasks crammed into one session |
    71→| `late_context` | Critical requirements provided late that should have been mentioned upfront |
    72→| `no_planning` | Jumped straight into implementation without discussing the approach |
    73→| `good_structure` | Well-structured session with clear objectives and logical flow |
    74→
    75→Each trait includes a severity (high/medium/low), evidence from the conversation, and a suggestion for improvement.
    76→
    77→## Storage
    78→
    79→All insights are stored in the `insights` table in `~/.code-insights/data.db`. You can export them as Markdown from the **Export** page in the dashboard.
    80→
```

```
     1→---
     2→title: "Sessions & Chat"
     3→description: "Browse sessions and view full conversation history."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## Session list
     7→
     8→The **Sessions** page shows all synced sessions with:
     9→
    10→- Session title (auto-generated from conversation content)
    11→- Source tool badge (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, Copilot CLI)
    12→- Date and duration
    13→- Message count and tool call count
    14→- Session character (classification)
    15→- Analysis status (analyzed / not analyzed)
    16→
    17→## Session characters
    18→
    19→Each session is classified into one of 7 types. Classification happens in two ways:
    20→
    21→1. **During sync** — a heuristic based on tool call patterns provides an initial classification
    22→2. **During LLM analysis** — the LLM classifies based on conversation content, providing more accurate results
    23→
    24→The LLM classification overrides the heuristic when analysis is run.
    25→
    26→| Character | Description |
    27→|-----------|-------------|
    28→| `deep_focus` | Long, concentrated work on a specific problem or area |
    29→| `bug_hunt` | Debugging-driven — investigating errors, tracing issues, fixing bugs |
    30→| `feature_build` | Building new functionality — creating files, adding endpoints, wiring components |
    31→| `exploration` | Research-oriented — reading code, searching, understanding before acting |
    32→| `refactor` | Restructuring existing code — renaming, moving, reorganizing without new features |
    33→| `learning` | Knowledge-seeking — asking questions, understanding concepts, getting explanations |
    34→| `quick_task` | Short and focused — small fix, config change, or one-off task |
    35→
    36→## Session detail
    37→
    38→Click any session to open the detail view with two tabs:
    39→
    40→### Overview tab
    41→
    42→- **Summary** — AI-generated narrative with outcome status and key bullets (requires analysis)
    43→- **Pull Requests** — GitHub PR links referenced in the conversation, shown as clickable badges
    44→- **Session Vitals** — duration, message count, tool calls, character type
    45→- **Usage Stats** — token counts, model used, estimated cost (collapsible)
    46→- **Insights** — learnings, decisions, and prompt quality cards (requires analysis)
    47→
    48→### Conversation tab
    49→
    50→- All messages in chronological order
    51→- Tool calls shown inline with parameters and results
    52→- Thinking blocks (Claude Code only)
    53→- Code blocks with syntax highlighting
    54→
    55→## Searching sessions
    56→
    57→Use the search bar on the Sessions page to find sessions by title or project name. The search is client-side and instant.
    58→
    59→## Filtering
    60→
    61→Filter sessions by:
    62→
    63→- **Project** — grouped by git remote or path
    64→- **Character** — session type
    65→- **Analysis status** — analyzed or not analyzed
    66→- **Source tool** — Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, Copilot CLI
    67→
    68→## Renaming sessions
    69→
    70→The auto-generated title can be customized. Click the pencil icon next to the title in the detail view to rename it. When analysis is run, the AI may also suggest a better title — you can apply or dismiss the suggestion.
    71→
```

     1→---
     2→title: "Analytics & Export"
     3→description: "Charts, trends, and Markdown export of your session data."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## Analytics page
     7→
     8→The **Analytics** page provides charts of your coding activity:
     9→
    10→- **Activity over time** — sessions per day/week
    11→- **Model usage** — sessions and messages by model
    12→- **Session characters** — distribution of session types
    13→- **Cost trends** — spending over time
    14→- **Tool call patterns** — which tools you use most
    15→
    16→## Time range
    17→
    18→All analytics charts respond to the period selector: 7 days, 30 days, 90 days, or all time.
    19→
    20→## Export
    21→
    22→The **Export** page lets you download your insights as Markdown:
    23→
    24→- **All insights** — every decision, learning, technique, and summary
    25→- **By project** — filtered to a single project
    26→- **By type** — just decisions, or just learnings, etc.
    27→
    28→The exported Markdown is compatible with Obsidian, Notion, Logseq, and any plain text editor.
    29→
    30→### Export format
    31→
    32→```markdown
    33→# Code Insights Export
    34→Generated: 2026-02-28
    35→
    36→## Decisions
    37→
    38→### Use SQLite instead of Firestore

    42→
    43→## Learnings
    44→
    45→### Next.js App Router catch-all routes
    46→*From: Web Repo — 2026-02-10*
    47→
    48→Use `[[...slug]]` for optional catch-all routes...

     1→---
     2→title: "Terminal Analytics"
     3→description: "Quick stats in your terminal using the stats command suite."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## Overview
     7→
     8→```bash
     9→code-insights stats
    10→```
    11→
    12→Shows a dashboard overview for the last 7 days: session count, messages, tool calls, duration, cost, and an activity chart.
    13→
    14→## Subcommands
    15→
    16→### Cost breakdown
    17→
    18→```bash
    19→code-insights stats cost
    20→```
    21→
    22→Cost breakdown by project and model. Shows token usage, cache hits, and USD cost.
    23→
    24→### Per-project details
    25→
    26→```bash
    27→code-insights stats projects
    28→```
    29→
    30→Detail cards for each project: session count, duration, character distribution.
    31→
    32→### Today's sessions
    33→
    34→```bash
    35→code-insights stats today
    36→```
    37→
    38→All sessions from today with titles and durations.
    39→
    40→### Model usage
    41→
    42→```bash
    43→code-insights stats models
    44→```
    45→
    46→Model usage distribution: session count, message count, and cost per model.
    47→
    48→## Shared flags
    49→
    50→All stats subcommands support these flags:
    51→
    52→| Flag | Default | Description |
    53→|------|---------|-------------|
    54→| `--period 7d\|30d\|90d\|all` | `7d` | Time range |
    55→| `--project <name>` | — | Filter to a specific project |
    56→| `--source <tool>` | — | Filter by source tool |
    57→| `--no-sync` | — | Skip auto-sync before showing stats |
    58→
    59→## Examples
    60→
    61→```bash
    62→# Cost for the last 30 days
    63→code-insights stats cost --period 30d
    64→
    65→# Today's sessions for a specific project
    66→code-insights stats today --project my-app
    67→
    68→# Model usage for Cursor sessions only
    69→code-insights stats models --source cursor
    70→```
    71→
```

```
     1→---
     2→title: "Introduction"
     3→description: "What is Code Insights and why it exists."
     4→---
     5→
     6→**Code Insights** is a free, open-source CLI tool and embedded dashboard for analyzing AI coding sessions.
     7→
     8→It parses session history from multiple AI coding tools — Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, Copilot CLI, and VS Code Copilot Chat — stores structured data in a local SQLite database, and provides both terminal analytics and a browser-based dashboard with LLM-powered insights.
     9→
    10→## Architecture
    11→
    12→```
    13→Session files → CLI → SQLite (~/.code-insights/data.db)
    14→                               ↓                    ↓
    15→                        Dashboard                Terminal Stats
    16→                     (localhost:7890)       (code-insights stats)
    17→```
    18→
    19→## What you get
    20→
    21→- **Session browser** — browse and search your AI coding sessions
    22→- **AI-powered insights** — extract decisions, learnings, techniques, and prompt quality scores
    23→- **Analytics charts** — activity over time, model usage, cost breakdown
    24→- **Markdown export** — export insights to Obsidian, Notion, or plain files
    25→- **Terminal stats** — `code-insights stats` for quick metrics without opening the browser
    26→
    27→## Supported tools
    28→
    29→| Tool | Auto-detected from |
    30→|------|--------------------|
    31→| Claude Code | `~/.claude/projects/**/*.jsonl` |
    32→| Cursor | Platform workspace storage (macOS/Linux/Windows) |
    33→| Codex CLI | `~/.codex/sessions/` |
    34→| Copilot CLI | `~/.copilot/session-state/` |
    35→| VS Code Copilot Chat | VS Code `workspaceStorage/` chat sessions |
    36→
    37→## Privacy
    38→
    39→Everything runs locally. No accounts, no cloud services, no data leaves your machine. The database lives at `~/.code-insights/data.db` and is plain SQLite — you can open it with any SQLite browser.
    40→
    41→## Next steps
    42→
    43→Continue to [Installation](/docs/getting-started/installation) to get up and running in 60 seconds.
    44→
```

     1→<p align="center">
     2→  <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/melagiri/code-insights/master/docs/assets/logo.svg" width="80" height="80" alt="Code Insights logo" />
     3→</p>
     4→
     5→<h1 align="center">Code Insights CLI</h1>
     6→
     7→Analyze AI coding sessions from the terminal. Parses session history from Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, and Copilot CLI — stores everything in a local SQLite database — and serves a built-in browser dashboard.
     8→
     9→**Local-first. No accounts. No cloud. No data leaves your machine.**
    10→
    11→## Install
    12→
    13→```bash
    14→npm install -g @code-insights/cli
    15→```
    16→
    17→Verify:
    18→
    19→```bash
    20→code-insights --version
    21→```
    22→
    23→## Quick Start
    24→
    25→```bash
    26→# 1. Initialize (creates ~/.code-insights/ config and database)
    27→code-insights init
    28→
    29→# 2. Sync sessions from all detected AI tools
    30→code-insights sync
    31→
    32→# 3. Open the built-in dashboard
    33→code-insights dashboard
    34→```
    35→
    36→The dashboard opens at `http://localhost:7890` and shows your sessions, analytics, and LLM-powered insights.
    37→
    38→## Supported Tools
    39→
    40→| Tool | Data Location |
    41→|------|---------------|
    42→| **Claude Code** | `~/.claude/projects/**/*.jsonl` |
    43→| **Cursor** | Workspace storage SQLite (macOS, Linux, Windows) |
    44→| **Codex CLI** | `~/.codex/sessions/YYYY/MM/DD/rollout-*.jsonl` |
    45→| **Copilot CLI** | `~/.copilot/session-state/{id}/events.jsonl` |
    46→
    47→Sessions from all tools are discovered automatically during sync.
    48→
    49→## Dashboard
    50→
    51→```bash
    52→code-insights dashboard
    53→```
    54→
    55→Opens the built-in React dashboard at `http://localhost:7890`. The dashboard provides:
    56→
    57→- **Session Browser** — search, filter, and view full session details
    58→- **Analytics** — usage patterns, cost trends, activity charts
    59→- **LLM Insights** — AI-generated summaries, decisions (with reasoning and trade-offs), learnings (with root cause analysis), prompt quality scoring with session traits
    60→- **Settings** — configure your LLM provider for analysis
    61→
    62→### Options
    63→
    64→```bash
    65→code-insights dashboard --port 8080    # Custom port
    66→code-insights dashboard --no-open      # Start server without opening browser
    67→```
    68→
    69→## CLI Commands
    70→
    71→### Setup & Configuration
    72→
    73→```bash
    74→# Interactive setup — prompts for Claude dir, excluded projects, etc.
    75→code-insights init
    76→
    77→# Show current configuration
    78→code-insights config
    79→
    80→# Configure LLM provider for session analysis (interactive)
    81→code-insights config llm
    82→
    83→# Configure LLM provider with flags (non-interactive)
    84→code-insights config llm --provider anthropic --model claude-sonnet-4-20250514 --api-key sk-ant-...
    85→
    86→# Show current LLM configuration
    87→code-insights config llm --show
    88→
    89→# Set a config value (e.g., disable telemetry)
    90→code-insights config set telemetry false
    91→```
    92→
    93→### Sync
    94→
    95→```bash
    96→# Sync new and modified sessions (incremental)
    97→code-insights sync
    98→
    99→# Force re-sync all sessions
   100→code-insights sync --force
   101→
   102→# Preview what would be synced (no changes made)
   103→code-insights sync --dry-run
   104→
   105→# Sync only from a specific tool
   106→code-insights sync --source cursor
   107→code-insights sync --source claude-code
   108→code-insights sync --source codex-cli
   109→code-insights sync --source copilot-cli
   110→
   111→# Sync only sessions from a specific project
   112→code-insights sync --project "my-project"
   113→
   114→# Quiet mode (useful for hooks)
   115→code-insights sync -q
   116→
   117→# Show diagnostic warnings from providers
   118→code-insights sync --verbose
   119→
   120→# Regenerate titles for all sessions
   121→code-insights sync --regenerate-titles
   122→```
   123→
   124→### Terminal Analytics
   125→
   126→```bash
   127→# Overview: sessions, cost, activity (last 7 days)
   128→code-insights stats
   129→
   130→# Cost breakdown by project and model
   131→code-insights stats cost
   132→
   133→# Per-project detail cards
   134→code-insights stats projects
   135→
   136→# Today's sessions with time, cost, and model details
   137→code-insights stats today
   138→
   139→# Model usage distribution and cost chart
   140→code-insights stats models
   141→```
   142→
   143→**Shared flags for all `stats` subcommands:**
   144→
   145→| Flag | Description |
   146→|------|-------------|
   147→| `--period 7d\|30d\|90d\|all` | Time range (default: `7d`) |
   148→| `--project <name>` | Scope to a specific project (fuzzy matching) |
   149→| `--source <tool>` | Filter by source tool |
   150→| `--no-sync` | Skip auto-sync before displaying stats |
   151→
   152→### Status & Maintenance
   153→
   154→```bash
   155→# Show sync statistics (sessions, projects, last sync)
   156→code-insights status
   157→
   158→# Open the local dashboard in your browser
   159→code-insights open
   160→code-insights open --project           # Open filtered to the current project
   161→
   162→# Delete all local data and reset sync state
   163→code-insights reset --confirm
   164→```
   165→
   166→### Auto-Sync Hook
   167→
   168→```bash
   169→# Install a Claude Code hook — auto-syncs when sessions end
   170→code-insights install-hook
   171→
   172→# Remove the hook
   173→code-insights uninstall-hook
   174→```
   175→
   176→### Telemetry
   177→
   178→Anonymous usage telemetry is opt-out. No PII is collected.
   179→
   180→```bash
   181→code-insights telemetry status   # Check current status
   182→code-insights telemetry disable  # Disable telemetry
   183→code-insights telemetry enable   # Re-enable telemetry
   184→```
   185→
   186→Alternatively, set the environment variable:
   187→
   188→```bash
   189→CODE_INSIGHTS_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 code-insights sync
   190→```
   191→
   192→## LLM Configuration
   193→
   194→Session analysis (summaries, decisions, learnings) requires an LLM provider. Configure it via CLI or the dashboard Settings page.
   195→
   196→```bash
   197→code-insights config llm
   198→```
   199→
   200→**Supported providers:**
   201→
   202→| Provider | Models | Requires API Key |
   203→|----------|--------|-----------------|
   204→| Anthropic | claude-opus-4-6, claude-sonnet-4-6, etc. | Yes |
   205→| OpenAI | gpt-4o, gpt-4o-mini, etc. | Yes |
   206→| Google Gemini | gemini-2.0-flash, gemini-2.0-pro, etc. | Yes |
   207→| Ollama | llama3.2, qwen2.5-coder, etc. | No (local) |
   208→
   209→API keys are stored in `~/.code-insights/config.json` (mode 0o600, readable only by you).
   210→
   211→## Development
   212→
   213→This is a pnpm workspace monorepo with three packages: `cli`, `dashboard`, and `server`.
   214→
   215→```bash
   216→# Clone
   217→git clone https://github.com/melagiri/code-insights.git
   218→cd code-insights
   219→
   220→# Install all dependencies
   221→pnpm install
   222→
   223→# Build all packages
   224→pnpm build
   225→
   226→# Link CLI for local testing
   227→cd cli && npm link
   228→code-insights --version
   229→
   230→# Watch mode (CLI only)
   231→cd cli && pnpm dev
   232→```
   233→
   234→### Workspace Structure
   235→
   236→```
   237→code-insights/
   238→├── cli/        # This package — Node.js CLI, SQLite, providers
   239→├── dashboard/  # Vite + React SPA
   240→└── server/     # Hono API server (serves dashboard + REST API)
   241→```
   242→
   243→### Contributing
   244→
   245→See [CONTRIBUTING.md](https://github.com/melagiri/code-insights/blob/master/CONTRIBUTING.md) for code style, PR guidelines, and how to add a new source tool provider.
   246→
   247→## Privacy
   248→
   249→- All session data is stored in `~/.code-insights/data.db` (SQLite) on your machine
   250→- No cloud accounts required
   251→- No data is transmitted anywhere (unless you explicitly use an LLM provider with a remote API key)
   252→- Anonymous telemetry collects only aggregate usage counts — no session content, no file paths
   253→
   254→## License
   255→
   256→MIT — see [LICENSE](https://github.com/melagiri/code-insights/blob/master/LICENSE) for details.
   257→
```

     1→---
     2→title: "Sessions & Chat"
     3→description: "Browse sessions and view full conversation history."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## Session list
     7→
     8→The **Sessions** page shows all synced sessions with:
     9→
    10→- Session title (auto-generated from conversation content)
    11→- Source tool badge (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, Copilot CLI)
    12→- Date and duration
    13→- Message count and tool call count
    14→- Session character (classification)
    15→- Analysis status (analyzed / not analyzed)
    16→
    17→<picture>
    18→  <source media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" srcSet="/images/session-insight-dark.png" />
    19→  <img src="/images/session-insight-light.png" alt="Session detail — insights tab" />
    20→</picture>
    21→
    22→## Session characters
    23→
    24→Each session is classified into one of 7 types. Classification happens in two ways:
    25→
    26→1. **During sync** — a heuristic based on tool call patterns provides an initial classification
    27→2. **During LLM analysis** — the LLM classifies based on conversation content, providing more accurate results
    28→
    29→The LLM classification overrides the heuristic when analysis is run.
    30→
    31→| Character | Description |
    32→|-----------|-------------|
    33→| `deep_focus` | Long, concentrated work on a specific problem or area |
    34→| `bug_hunt` | Debugging-driven — investigating errors, tracing issues, fixing bugs |
    35→| `feature_build` | Building new functionality — creating files, adding endpoints, wiring components |
    36→| `exploration` | Research-oriented — reading code, searching, understanding before acting |
    37→| `refactor` | Restructuring existing code — renaming, moving, reorganizing without new features |
    38→| `learning` | Knowledge-seeking — asking questions, understanding concepts, getting explanations |
    39→| `quick_task` | Short and focused — small fix, config change, or one-off task |
    40→
    41→## Session detail
    42→
    43→Click any session to open the detail view with two tabs:
    44→
    45→### Overview tab
    46→
    47→- **Summary** — AI-generated narrative with outcome status and key bullets (requires analysis)
    48→- **Pull Requests** — GitHub PR links referenced in the conversation, shown as clickable badges
    49→- **Session Vitals** — duration, message count, tool calls, character type
    50→- **Usage Stats** — token counts, model used, estimated cost (collapsible)
    51→- **Insights** — learnings, decisions, and prompt quality cards (requires analysis)
    52→
    53→### Conversation tab
    54→
    55→<picture>
    56→  <source media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" srcSet="/images/session-chat-dark.png" />
    57→  <img src="/images/session-chat-light.png" alt="Session conversation view" />
    58→</picture>
    59→
    60→- All messages in chronological order
    61→- Tool calls shown inline with parameters and results
    62→- Thinking blocks (Claude Code only)
    63→- Code blocks with syntax highlighting
    64→
    65→## Searching sessions
    66→
    67→Use the search bar on the Sessions page to find sessions by title or project name. The search is client-side and instant.
    68→
    69→## Filtering
    70→
    71→Filter sessions by:
    72→
    73→- **Project** — grouped by git remote or path
    74→- **Character** — session type
    75→- **Analysis status** — analyzed or not analyzed
    76→- **Source tool** — Claude Code, Cursor, Codex CLI, Copilot CLI
    77→
    78→## Renaming sessions
    79→
    80→The auto-generated title can be customized. Click the pencil icon next to the title in the detail view to rename it. When analysis is run, the AI may also suggest a better title — you can apply or dismiss the suggestion.
    81→
