id: toolu_013EBeiBfFArtEWwovPCtcZ1
```
     1→---
     2→title: "Insights & Analysis"
     3→description: "LLM-powered analysis of your coding sessions."
     4→---
     5→
     6→## What insights are
     7→
     8→When you analyze a session, the CLI server sends the conversation to your configured LLM provider and extracts structured insights. These are stored in the local SQLite database and displayed in the dashboard.
     9→
    10→The analysis uses a "senior staff engineer writing for a knowledge base" persona — each insight is written as if a future developer will read it 6 months from now with no context about the session.
    11→
    12→<picture>
    13→  <source media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" srcSet="/images/insights-dark.png" />
    14→  <img src="/images/insights-light.png" alt="Insights page" />
    15→</picture>
    16→
    17→## The 5 insight types
    18→
    19→| Type | What it captures |
    20→|------|-----------------|
    21→| **Summary** | Narrative of what was accomplished, outcome status (success/partial/abandoned/blocked), and key artifacts changed |
    22→| **Decision** | A technical choice with full context: situation, reasoning, rejected alternatives, trade-offs, and conditions for revisiting |
    23→| **Learning** | A technical discovery: observable symptom, root cause, transferable takeaway, and when it applies |
    24→| **Technique** | A reusable approach or pattern demonstrated |
    25→| **Prompt Quality** | Efficiency score, wasted turns, anti-patterns, and session behavioral traits |
    26→
    27→### Decision structure
    28→
    29→Each decision insight includes:
    30→
    31→- **Situation** — what problem or requirement led to the decision
    32→- **Choice** — what was chosen and how it was implemented
    33→- **Reasoning** — the key factors that tipped the decision
    34→- **Alternatives** — rejected options with reasons why
    35→- **Trade-offs** — what downsides were accepted
    36→- **Revisit when** — conditions under which to reconsider
    37→
    38→### Learning structure
    39→
    40→Each learning insight includes:
    41→
    42→- **Symptom** — what went wrong or was confusing
    43→- **Root cause** — the underlying technical reason
    44→- **Takeaway** — the transferable lesson for similar situations
    45→- **Applies when** — conditions under which this knowledge is relevant
    46→
    47→## Running analysis
    48→
    49→1. Open the dashboard: `code-insights dashboard`
    50→2. Navigate to **Sessions**
    51→3. Click into any session
    52→4. Click **Analyze** (requires LLM configured in Settings)
    53→
    54→Analysis runs server-side via the Hono API. Progress is streamed back to the dashboard in real time via Server-Sent Events (SSE), so you can see each insight as it's extracted. Your API key stays on your machine and is never sent anywhere else.
    55→
    56→During analysis, the LLM also classifies the session into one of 7 character types (deep_focus, bug_hunt, feature_build, etc.) and writes it back to the session record.
    57→
    58→## Bulk analysis
    59→
    60→From the **Dashboard** page, click **Analyze** on the unanalyzed sessions card to run analysis across all pending sessions. You can also analyze individual sessions from the session detail page. Progress is shown in real time via SSE streaming.
    61→
    62→## Prompt quality analysis
    63→
    64→The prompt quality analysis evaluates your prompting efficiency on a 0-100 scale.
    65→
    66→### What it detects
    67→
    68→- **Wasted turns** — messages that led to unnecessary clarifications or corrections, with the original message, what went wrong, and a suggested rewrite
    69→- **Anti-patterns** — recurring bad habits (e.g., vague instructions, missing file paths) with specific fixes
    70→- **Session traits** — higher-level behavioral patterns about how the session was structured:
    71→
    72→| Trait | What it means |
    73→|-------|--------------|
    74→| `context_drift` | Session covers too many unrelated objectives, causing the AI to lose context |
    75→| `objective_bloat` | Too many different tasks crammed into one session |
    76→| `late_context` | Critical requirements provided late that should have been mentioned upfront |
    77→| `no_planning` | Jumped straight into implementation without discussing the approach |
    78→| `good_structure` | Well-structured session with clear objectives and logical flow |
    79→
    80→Each trait includes a severity (high/medium/low), evidence from the conversation, and a suggestion for improvement.
    81→
    82→## Storage
    83→
    84→All insights are stored in the `insights` table in `~/.code-insights/data.db`. You can export them as Markdown from the **Export** page in the dashboard.
    85→
```
